## LEGH-1401 [负面]
判据: The response treats Nadia's November 2023 will bequest (leave my half to my daughter) as evidence of "course of dealing" even though course of dealing is defined in Hansen Estate V. Hansen (2012 ONCA) as a pattern of mutual, consistent behaviour over time showing that both parties treat their interests as separate (separate tax filings and property-management decisions). A single will or one-off email is insufficient.
提示: Fails wil bequest trap
出处: prbench:505877d4a5df64a056db15ee | 话题: Real Estate & Property Law

## LEGH-1402 [必须]
判据: Explains that the right of survivorship happens when one joint tenant dies: their interest passes to the survivor, not their estate.
提示: Foundational doctrine
出处: prbench:505877d4a5df64a056db15ee | 话题: Real Estate & Property Law

## LEGH-1403 [必须]
判据: Explains that a unilateral act must be a completed act operating on the owner's share, such as a registered transfer, mortgage, or sale, not merely a declaration of intent.
提示: defines unilateral act
出处: prbench:505877d4a5df64a056db15ee | 话题: Real Estate & Property Law

## LEGH-1404 [必须]
判据: Cites Hansen Estate v. Hansen (2012 ONCA) as the clarifying authority for the course-of-dealing test.
提示: Core modern ontairo framework.
出处: prbench:505877d4a5df64a056db15ee | 话题: Real Estate & Property Law

## LEGH-1405 [负面]
判据: The response conflates or blends the mutual agreement test (explicit present consent between the parties) with the course-of-dealings test (reciprocal conduct implying consent).
提示: fails to separate methods
出处: prbench:505877d4a5df64a056db15ee | 话题: Real Estate & Property Law

## LEGH-1406 [必须]
判据: Lists all three severance methods from Williams v. Hensman (unilateral act, mutual agreement, course of dealing).
提示: Core legal framework

## LEGH-1564 [负面]
判据: The response incorrectly states that litigation privilege applies only to documents created after litigation has formally commenced. The correct rule is that the privilege covers documents created when litigation was the dominant purpose for their creation and was in reasonable contemplation, not merely after it had started.
提示: Misstating these core principles would lead to an flawed strategy for protecting the document.
出处: prbench:60e8ee630db78dfe84d3e12c | 话题: Litigation & Dispute Resolution

## LEGH-1662 [负面]
判据: The response claims that representing a close friend is automatically prohibited under the conduct rules (without conditions). Example of a violation: “It is absolutely forbidden to represent a friend.”.

## LEGH-1664 [必须]
判据: The response refers to the advocate-witness rule, for example: “If you may be a material witness, you should not appear as an advocate.”
提示: This rule directly applies to friendships where social conversations may later make the lawyer a witness. A complete response must flag this prohibition to prevent ethical breaches and wasted resources.
出处: prbench:68fce91b6eb30d1c48b03d58 | 话题: Legal Ethics, Compliance & Risk Management

## LEGH-1665 [必须]
判据: The response states that in NSW solicitors owe a paramount duty to the court, for example, “Rule 3 requires solicitors to place duty to the court above client interests.”
提示: This is the foundational duty under the NSW conduct rules. A response that omits or misstates it risks presenting an incomplete or misleading picture of professional obligations. Without this recognition, the analysis loses legitimacy in the eyes of courts, insurers, and regulators.
出处: prbench:68fce91b6eb30d1c48b03d58 | 话题: Legal Ethics, Compliance & Risk Management

## LEGH-1666 [必须]
判据: The response describes the “own-interest conflict” rule by explaining that acting for a close friend risks impairing objectivity, for example: “Rule 12.1 prohibits acting if personal interests, such as friendship, may conflict with client duties.”
提示: Identifying the own-interest conflict is critical because it is the main reason a lawyer might need to decline representation here. It demonstrates precise knowledge of the ASCR and shows the ability to translate black-letter rules into practical risk awareness.
出处: prbench:68fce91b6eb30d1c48b03d58 | 话题: Legal Ethics, Compliance & Risk Management

## LEGH-1667 [必须]
判据: The response explains that even if no actual conflict exists, the appearance of conflict can undermine public confidence, for example, by describing the “fair-minded observer” test.

## LEGH-2142 [负面]
判据: The response suggests that back pay can include "foreseeable direct costs" (e.g., medical or late fees caused by the unlawful firing) as a common remedy.
提示: While the NLRB has tried to expand make-whole relief (Thryv decision), the inclusion of "foreseeable direct costs" remains highly contested and not a common, settled, or guaranteed remedy enforced by courts. Including this as a "Common remedy" is misleading about the realistic outcome and overpromises damages.

## LEGH-2143 [必须]

提示: The criterion adds practical utility. In a retaliation case (ULP), the burden of proof is technical (Wright Line test). The response must list the evidentiary elements to guide the employee on what evidence is needed.

提示: Merges original #1 and #7. The original #1 (state-by-state) was irrelevant, as the primary law (NLRA) is federal. The original #7 (“different industries”) was vague and failed in Objectivity. This revision makes the criterion objective, precise, and relevant to the topic, focusing on the correct distinction (private vs. public vs. RLA) that was identified as a failure of Collective Exhaustion.

## LEGH-2145 [建议]
判据: The response correctly identifies the federal agency that handles private-sector union retaliation charges as the National Labor Relations Board (NLRB).
提示: Identifying the correct agency is foundational, as union retaliation cases fall under the NLRB and not the common wrongful termination system (which might direct the employee to the DOL or EEOC). Without this criterion, the response could fail on the most critical procedural point.

## LEGH-2146 [必须]
判据: The response explicitly clarifies the distinction between an NLRA Unfair Labor Practice (for union activity, filed with NLRB) and a discrimination claim (for protected class status, filed with EEOC).
提示: The prompt is in a union context, but many responses stray into EEOC or Title VII. The distinction ensures the employee understands why the NLRB is the correct forum and prevents them from mixing up statutes and deadlines.
出处: prbench:9e3d672a1c16c2cfe83670eb | 话题: Labor & Employment Law

## LEGH-2147 [负面]
判据: The response suggests that a union can "represent you" in the NLRB process, which conflates the union's role with that of the NLRB's General Counsel (GC).
提示: The NLRB's GC is the exclusive public prosecutor. While a union can file the charge, advise, and provide witnesses, they do not legally represent the employee in the NLRB hearing; the GC's attorney does. This misstatement confuses the employee about who holds legal responsibility.
出处: prbench:9e3d672a1c16c2cfe83670eb | 话题: Labor & Employment Law

## LEGH-2148 [负面]
判据: The response includes a list of NLRA exclusions (e.g., domestic workers, agricultural workers, rail/airline workers) that omits the critical exclusion of "independent contractors," leading to an incomplete and potentially dangerous legal assessment of employee status.
提示: While domestic workers are excluded, simply listing them is insufficient. The most common critical error in NLRA exclusions is the omission of "independent contractors," which is the most frequent dispute. Response A omits independent contractors, thus providing incomplete and potentially harmful advice to a worker misclassified as such. This criterion penalizes the failure to include the most critical omission for a complete list of NLRA exclusions.
出处: prbench:9e3d672a1c16c2cfe83670eb | 话题: Labor & Employment Law
